about us

Quality Construction.

DH Construction Inc. is an insurance-related reconstruction business for residential and commercial properties specializing in Large-Loss Claims.

Our full-time team of dedicated staff, including 50+ in-house skilled trades people, handle over 300 projects a year, providing us the expertise, resources, and experience to respond quickly and take charge of your damaged property efficiently.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/qual_con_num1a.png)

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/12/image_layers_1-2.png)

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/qual_con_num2.png)

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/12/ralph.jpg "ralph")

our ceo

Ralph Dinwiddie

Ralph received his General Contractor’s License from the state of CA in 1978. After witnessing the quality of his workmanship and character, the insurance industry approached him, requesting he attend their insurance training program. After completing the program, DH Construction was founded in 1992, specializing in insurance re-construction.

#### Ralph Dinwiddie

Founding Partner / President / Chairman of the Board

our services

Rebuilding Lives Since 1992

Whether an incident impacts your home or business, we are there from start to finish, dedicated to quickly restoring your property so your life can get back to normal. Where other builders may delay, we hit the ground running by immediately boarding up and securing your home from any further damage and communicating with your insurance company for approvals on the scope of repairs.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/hammer1.png)

##### Emergency & Board Up

DH Construction is contracted with multiple fire & police departments in our coverage area to be on call 24/7, every day of the year. We respond within 1 hour to most locations for these emergency services. After a fire or other disaster, a property is often left with gaping openings in the structure. We have dedicated vehicles fully stocked with necessary supplies to secure the property and prevent further damage/theft.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/firedamage.png)

##### Fire Damage

Destruction caused by fire can be rapid and brutal. What isn’t completely destroyed reeks of smoke and is covered in filthy soot. Our experienced Estimators will explain all your options and help expedite your insurance claim so you’re on the path to recovery in the quickest way possible.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/waterpipedamage.png)

##### Water Damage

From a burst pipe to fire suppression efforts, water can be as damaging as fire and can create additional problems, including dangerous mold. Rapid remediation is an essential part of the process to prevent further damage. Allow DH Construction to assist in getting the process started and repair the damage created by water.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/winddamage.png)

##### Wind Damage

Tree impact to your structure, broken windows, damaged siding and destroyed roofing can all be caused by wind. With our 24/7/365 emergency services, we’ll quickly secure your property by making temporary repairs to prevent further damage as the first steps towards more permanent restoration.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/icesnowdamage.png)

##### Ice & Snow Damage

In higher elevations with more severe climate, ice and snow can commonly cause property damage. With seasonally occupied homes, this can go undetected for long periods of time and worsen the damage. Our experience with these specific circumstances will result in your best outcome on your insurance settlement so we can complete the necessary repairs.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/vehicledamage.png)

##### Vehicle Impact Damage

This type of accident can damage your property’s structure, fencing, or any other point of impact area. As unexpected as it may be, our experts can assess the specific damage and help your insurance adjuster complete a scope of work to remedy the destruction.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/haildamage.png)

##### Hail Damage

Balls of ice hurled from the sky can break windows, destroy roofs, and damage siding. DH Construction is ready to remedy this damage quickly & efficiently by effectively working with your insurance adjuster to correct the situation back to normal.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/burglardamage.png)

##### Vandalism & Burglary

Being violated by illegal activity can result in different emotional responses than those caused by naturally occurring events. We have assessed this damage many times and can guide you smoothly through the process of returning your property to wholeness.

testimonials

What Our Clients Say

“I cannot begin to say how pleased we are with DH Construction. I wish I could load the photo of my 2 story house vs. an 80+ foot eucalyptus tree. We are just finally getting to a point where rebuilding can begin but every step of the way they have been amazing. I would recommend them in a heartbeat. Having grown up in a huge construction family, I'm picky.”

![Christine Keith-Cribb photo](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/testimonial_ckc-100x100.jpg)

### Christine Keith-Cribb

Facebook

“DH Construction is the real deal. If you need construction work done with honesty and integrity then this firm has it in spades. Anyone can put on tool belt and have a pickup and call themselves a construction firm but let's face it, there are a lot of flakes out there. Everyone at DH Construction treats you like family and is there for you no matter what. They put our home back together after a flood and stood by all their work no questions asked.”

![Michael Cuccia photo](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/testimonial_mc-100x100.jpg)

### Michael Cuccia

Facebook

“What a pleasure to work with such a professional group! DH Construction relieved me of all the worry and hassles, and turned a bad situation into a beautifully reconstructed home. I can't thank you enough.”

![Susan Trapnell photo](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/testimonial_st-100x100.jpg)

### Susan Trapnell

Facebook

We had a house fire and our awesome insurance agent, Rick, immediately said to forgo everyone else and go with DH Construction. We worked with Jennifer and Ken who were wonderful, reassuring, witty but very professional and always responsive. Initially when DH and Grange's adjusters were far apart and we were freaking out, they reassured us everything would be worked out between them. We really appreciated the way they worked well together. Steve, our superintendent, has been absolutely fabulous and he worked very well with us. Throughout, he was very professional and provided explanations as to his decisions. The last month was where he earned his keep. He pushed all the contractors and reassured us we would be back in our home on time. He always double checked in before moving on with change orders to ensure there were no misunderstandings. The work done in the home is tasteful & looks very nice. We could not have asked for a better Superintendent and DH felt like family during these trying times. They have been great, in a world where construction companies have a bad reputation for not keeping timelines or following through. The communication from DH is outstanding!

![Charles Odipo photo](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/image-100x100.jpeg)

### Charles Odipo

about us

Quality Construction.

DH Construction Inc. is an insurance-related reconstruction business for residential and commercial properties specializing in Large-Loss Claims.

Our full-time team of dedicated staff, including 50+ in-house skilled trades people, handle over 300 projects a year, providing us the expertise, resources, and experience to respond quickly and take charge of your damaged property efficiently.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/qual_con_num1a.png)

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/12/image_layers_1-2.png)

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/qual_con_num2.png)

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/12/ralph.jpg "ralph")

our ceo

Ralph Dinwiddie

Ralph received his General Contractor’s License from the state of CA in 1978. After witnessing the quality of his workmanship and character, the insurance industry approached him, requesting he attend their insurance training program. After completing the program, DH Construction was founded in 1992, specializing in insurance re-construction.

our services

Rebuilding Lives Since 1992

Whether an incident impacts your home or business, we are there from start to finish, dedicated to quickly restoring your property so your life can get back to normal. Where other builders may delay, we hit the ground running by immediately boarding up and securing your home from any further damage and communicating with your insurance company for approvals on the scope of repairs.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/hammer1.png)

##### Emergency & Board Up

DH Construction is contracted with multiple fire & police departments in our coverage area to be on call 24/7, every day of the year. We respond within 1 hour to most locations for these emergency services. After a fire or other disaster, a property is often left with gaping openings in the structure. We have dedicated vehicles fully stocked with necessary supplies to secure the property and prevent further damage/theft.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/firedamage.png)

##### Fire Damage

Destruction caused by fire can be rapid and brutal. What isn’t completely destroyed reeks of smoke and is covered in filthy soot. Our experienced Estimators will explain all your options and help expedite your insurance claim so you’re on the path to recovery in the quickest way possible.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/waterpipedamage.png)

##### Water Damage

From a burst pipe to fire suppression efforts, water can be as damaging as fire and can create additional problems, including dangerous mold. Rapid remediation is an essential part of the process to prevent further damage. Allow DH Construction to assist in getting the process started and repair the damage created by water.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/winddamage.png)

##### Wind Damage

Tree impact to your structure, broken windows, damaged siding and destroyed roofing can all be caused by wind. With our 24/7/365 emergency services, we’ll quickly secure your property by making temporary repairs to prevent further damage as the first steps towards more permanent restoration.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/icesnowdamage.png)

##### Ice & Snow Damage

In higher elevations with more severe climate, ice and snow can commonly cause property damage. With seasonally occupied homes, this can go undetected for long periods of time and worsen the damage. Our experience with these specific circumstances will result in your best outcome on your insurance settlement so we can complete the necessary repairs.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/vehicledamage.png)

##### Vehicle Impact Damage

This type of accident can damage your property’s structure, fencing, or any other point of impact area. As unexpected as it may be, our experts can assess the specific damage and help your insurance adjuster complete a scope of work to remedy the destruction.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/haildamage.png)

##### Hail Damage

Balls of ice hurled from the sky can break windows, destroy roofs, and damage siding. DH Construction is ready to remedy this damage quickly & efficiently by effectively working with your insurance adjuster to correct the situation back to normal.

![](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/burglardamage.png)

##### Vandalism & Burglary

Being violated by illegal activity can result in different emotional responses than those caused by naturally occurring events. We have assessed this damage many times and can guide you smoothly through the process of returning your property to wholeness.

“I cannot begin to say how pleased we are with DH Construction. I wish I could load the photo of my 2 story house vs. an 80+ foot eucalyptus tree. We are just finally getting to a point where rebuilding can begin but every step of the way they have been amazing. I would recommend them in a heartbeat. Having grown up in a huge construction family, I'm picky.”

![Christine Keith-Cribb photo](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/testimonial_ckc-100x100.jpg)

### Christine Keith-Cribb

Facebook

“DH Construction is the real deal. If you need construction work done with honesty and integrity then this firm has it in spades. Anyone can put on tool belt and have a pickup and call themselves a construction firm but let's face it, there are a lot of flakes out there. Everyone at DH Construction treats you like family and is there for you no matter what. They put our home back together after a flood and stood by all their work no questions asked.”

![Michael Cuccia photo](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/testimonial_mc-100x100.jpg)

### Michael Cuccia

Facebook

“What a pleasure to work with such a professional group! DH Construction relieved me of all the worry and hassles, and turned a bad situation into a beautifully reconstructed home. I can't thank you enough.”

![Susan Trapnell photo](https://dhfirerestoration.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/03/testimonial_st-100x100.jpg)

### Susan Trapnell

Facebook

We had a house fire and our awesome insurance agent, Rick, immediately said to forgo everyone else and go with DH Construction. We worked with Jennifer and Ken who were wonderful, reassuring, witty but very professional and always responsive. Initially when DH and Grange's adjusters were far apart and we were freaking out, they reassured us everything would be worked out between them. We really appreciated the way they worked well together. Steve, our superintendent, has been absolutely fabulous and he worked very well with us. Throughout, he was very professional and provided explanations as to his decisions. The last month was where he earned his keep. He pushed all the contractors and reassured us we would be back in our home on time. He always double checked in before moving on with change orders to ensure there were no misunderstandings. The work done in the home is tasteful & looks very nice. We could not have asked for a better Superintendent and DH felt like family during these trying times. They have been great, in a world where construction companies have a bad reputation for not keeping timelines or following through. The communication from DH is outstanding!

# Your Home Is In Good Hands

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw== "house")

Residential

When disaster strikes your home, we know how it impacts all facets of your life. Our experienced team of professionals will be by your side to help assist you through any delays and obstacles.

### Emergency Response

We provide 24/7 emergency services to protect your property from further damage. These services include board up, plumbing, heating and electric, as well as other services to secure your home and property while it is vulnerable and awaiting repair.

### Claim Management

Our staff meets with your insurance company adjuster to determine the Scope of Damage and to prepare an estimate. We focus on your needs and specific issues, while working within the guidelines of your insurance company.

### Detailed Estimate

A detailed damage estimate is prepared for all costs from fire, water, and smoke. Our experienced estimators document all damage and anticipate hidden damage caused by water and smoke penetration.

### Working Together

We work with you every step of the way, from helping you prepare all necessary paperwork, getting your approval on repair specifications, and going through sampling materials. We will guide you through the process, keeping you fully informed along the way.

our services

Rapid remediation is an essential part of the process to prevent further damage. Allow DH Construction to assist in getting the process started and repair the damage created by water.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Wind Damage

Tree impact to your structure, broken windows, damaged siding and destroyed roofing can all be caused by wind. With our 24/7/365 emergency services, we’ll quickly secure your property by making temporary repairs to prevent further damage as the first steps towards more permanent restoration.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Ice & Snow Damage

In higher elevations with more severe climate, ice and snow can commonly cause property damage. With seasonally occupied homes, this can go undetected for long periods of time and worsen the damage. Our experience with these specific circumstances will result in your best outcome on your insurance settlement so we can complete the necessary repairs.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Vehicle Impact Damage

This type of accident can damage your property’s structure, fencing, or any other point of impact area. As unexpected as it may be, our experts can assess the specific damage and help your insurance adjuster complete a scope of work to remedy the destruction.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Hail Damage

Balls of ice hurled from the sky can break windows, destroy roofs, and damage siding. DH Construction is ready to remedy this damage quickly & efficiently by effectively working with your insurance adjuster to correct the situation back to normal.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Vandalism & Burglary

Being violated by illegal activity can result in different emotional responses than those caused by naturally occurring events. We have assessed this damage many times and can guide you smoothly through the process of returning your property to wholeness.

We are licensed in California, northern Nevada and Reno. Our service area spans from Stockton to Redding and from Reno to Fairfield.

# Back To Business In No Time

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw== "commercial")

Commercial

When disaster strikes your business, it directly impacts your livelihood. DH Construction has hundreds of cumulative years of experience in every aspect of estimating, working with insurance companies and rebuilding your space. We’ll get you back to business.

### Emergency Response

We provide emergency services to protect your business from further damage. These services include board up, plumbing, heating and electric, as well as other services to secure your structure and property while it is vulnerable and awaiting repair.

### Claim Management

Our staff meets with your insurance company adjuster to agree on the Scope of Damage and to prepare an estimate. We coordinate with property management staff to focus on your needs and specific issues, while working within the guidelines of your insurance company.

### Detailed Estimate

A detailed damage estimate is prepared for all costs from fire, water, and smoke. Our experienced estimators document all damage and anticipate hidden damage caused by water and smoke penetration.

### Working Together

We work with you every step of the way, from helping you prepare all necessary paperwork, getting your approval on repair specifications, and going through sampling materials. We understand the urgency involved in getting your business reopened.

our services

Rebuilding Lives Since 1992

Whether an incident impacts your home or business, we are there from start to finish, dedicated to quickly restoring your property so your life can get back to normal. Where other builders may delay, we hit the ground running by immediately boarding up and securing your home from any further damage and communicating with your insurance company for approvals on the scope of repairs.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Emergency & Board Up

DH Construction is contracted with multiple fire & police departments in our coverage area to be on call 24/7, every day of the year. We respond within 1 hour to most locations for these emergency services. After a fire or other disaster, a property is often left with gaping openings in the structure. We have dedicated vehicles fully stocked with necessary supplies to secure the property and prevent further damage/theft.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Fire Damage

Destruction caused by fire can be rapid and brutal. What isn’t completely destroyed reeks of smoke and is covered in filthy soot. Our experienced Estimators will explain all your options and help expedite your insurance claim so you’re on the path to recovery in the quickest way possible.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Water Damage

From a burst pipe to fire suppression efforts, water can be as damaging as fire and can create additional problems, including dangerous mold. Rapid remediation is an essential part of the process to prevent further damage. Allow DH Construction to assist in getting the process started and repair the damage created by water.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

##### Wind Damage

Tree impact to your structure, broken windows, damaged siding and destroyed roofing can all be caused by wind. With our 24/7/365 emergency services, we’ll quickly secure your property by making temporary repairs to prevent further damage as the first steps towards more permanent restoration.

![](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)

In higher elevations with more severe climate, ice and snow can commonly cause property damage. With seasonally occupied homes, this can go undetected for long periods of time and worsen the damage. Our experience with these specific circumstances will result in your best outcome on your insurance settlement so we can complete the necessary repairs.

## Guaranteed Admission. _Promise_ of Free Tuition.

It's easier than ever to apply to any of our four student-centered colleges.

When you apply now, you guarantee your admission to Los Rios. After that, fill out the Free Application for Federal Student Aid (FAFSA) or California Dream Act Application (CADAA) to save money on tuition and other fees. It's as simple as that.

[Frequently Asked Questions about applying to college](https://arc.losrios.edu/apply-faq)

[

### Apply Now

Guarantee your admission to a Los Rios college.

→

](https://arc.losrios.edu/apply)[

### Save Money

Fill out the FAFSA or CADAA to qualify for financial aid.

→

](https://arc.losrios.edu/savemoney)

## Support for Undecided Students

We understand that choosing a major can be one of the most challenging decisions you'll face during your academic journey.

![ARC Homebases Undecided Student logo](arc/main/img/ARC03-Academics/ARC-Homebases/arc-homebases-undecided-735x414.jpeg)

If you feel uncertain or overwhelmed, you’re not alone. Our Undecided Student Support Program is here to help you navigate your options, explore your interests, and find a path that aligns with your passions and career goals.

## Ready to get started?

Reach out to our Undecided Student Support Program to schedule an appointment or learn more about our resources for undecided students. Together, we’ll explore your options and help you find the path that’s right for you.

[Support for Undecided Students](/academics/support-for-undecided-students "Support for Undecided Students Button")

## Indigenous Land Use Statement

We acknowledge the land which we occupy at American River College as the traditional home of the Nisenan, Maidu, and Miwok tribal nations. These sovereign people have been the caretakers of this land since time immemorial. Despite centuries of genocide and occupation the Nisenan, Maidu, and Miwok continue as vibrant and resilient tribes and bands, both Federally recognized and unrecognized. We take this opportunity to acknowledge the generations that have gone before as well as the present-day Nisenan, Maidu, and Miwok people.

## Stay in-the-know and make an impact

BOMA/BOMI supports property owners, managers, professionals, and service providers of all commercial building types with advocacy, influence, and education. We facilitate powerful connections and provide valuable resources, legislative advocacy, and professional credentials opportunities to help you exceed your goals and grow your company.

![](https://boma.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/BOMA-Connect.png "BOMA Connect")

### Connect

We are dedicated to being a future-focused advocate for professionals in the commercial real estate industry, connecting them with the best resources, partners, and expertise in the industry.

[](https://boma.org/connect-grow-lead-succeed/)

![](https://boma.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/BOMA-Grow.png "BOMA Grow")

### Grow

We know how fast this industry moves and how complicated it can be to navigate. We stay on the leading edge of trends, policies, and workforce development to keep you at the forefront of the industry.

[](https://boma.org/connect-grow-lead-succeed/)

![](https://boma.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/BOMA-Lead.png "BOMA Lead")

### Lead

We provide the tools necessary to lead the commercial real estate industry through committee participation, advocacy involvement, business tools, programs, and workforce development initiatives.

[](https://boma.org/connect-grow-lead-succeed/)

![](https://boma.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/BOMA-Succeed.png "BOMA Succeed")

### Succeed

Gain momentum in your career, expand your credentials, and exceed your professional goals alongside a supportive community.

[](https://boma.org/connect-grow-lead-succeed/)

### TESTIMONIALS

### Hear from our members

When you're part of a trade association such as BOMA, you invest not just in yourself but the community you serve. You can't be efficient if you don't surround yourself with experts.

\- Sandrena Robinson, Consultant, LBA Realty

In addition to the educational value, the networking opportunities at the conference were exceptional. Connecting with like-minded professionals, industry leaders, and peers expanded my professional network significantly.

\- Elizabeth Steffeter, Tenant Service Coordinator, Lincoln Property Company

\[BOMA 360\] is third-party verification for our investors, our customers, that we're running our properties at the highest level from an operating and management standpoint.

\- Melanie Colbert, Principal, Operations, LBA Realty

"Earning the RPA® designation was an important step in my overall career development, enabling me to assume additional roles and responsibilities. My participation in the program, from the courses, discussions, and readings, has broadened my understanding of commercial real estate and provided me with resources I continue using today.”

\- Michael Giese, RPA, BOMI-HP Executive Director, Glenstar

### CASA MISSION

Ensuring consistency and support for children in the foster care and juvenile justice systems through the use of volunteer advocates advancing the best interests of each child.

[ABOUT CASA SACRAMENTO](/who-we-are/)

### What People Are Saying

One of my best memories of being a CASA was getting my CASA child together with his siblings through the help of the other CASAs. I credit CASA for being there so that Child Protective Services could sanction the visits and help coordinate with the foster families.  We were lucky to have a tireless social worker on the case.

\- CASA Advocate

Foster children see so many therapists, social workers, foster parents, and others that come and go. There needs to be at least one person who is permanent and has the child's best interest at heart.

\- CASA Advocate

I was used to being the one who advocated for him, but that day in court, he took on the role himself.

\- CASA Advocate

There are many layers to being a CASA Volunteer. My CASA children are very vocal about what they want, and I help them sort through it all and present it to the court. I also try hard to make sure they get the resources they need like extra tutoring or the chance to be involved in sports.

\- CASA Advocate

#### How Does CASA Make A Difference?

CASA volunteers help maintain a sense of urgency to all children’s cases and recognize that permanency is essential to well being.

![Icon of two adults and a child from the waist up](https://sacramentocasa.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/icon3.png)

Number of youth in foster  
care in Sacramento County

1,099

(as of 1/1/2026)

![Icon of adult with arms around child](https://sacramentocasa.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/icon1.png)

Number of children served by  
CASA Sacramento volunteers

260

in 2024 & 2025

![Icon of two youths standing next to each other](https://sacramentocasa.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/icon2.png)

Number of youth currently  
waiting for a CASA

43

(as of 4/2026)

### SEE OUR WORK IN ACTION

CASA Sacramento’s vision is one in which all children and families impacted by the child welfare and juvenile justice systems have equitable access to the resources and support they need to thrive.

Become an Advocate

### Volunteer

Become A Court Appointed Special  
Advocate For A Child

[Register](/portal/volunteer-intake)

### Donate

Your Gift Makes A Difference  
In A Child's Life.

[Give Now](/donate-now/)

### MAKING A WORLD OF IMPACT

Committed, highly-trained volunteer Court Appointed Special Advocates (CASAs) are appointed by a judge to an individual child’s case, to act and advocate for the child’s best interests in court and in all aspects of their life, making a positive difference and giving them an opportunity to thrive. Through an intentional and culturally responsive lens, we train committed, consistent and caring adults who can change the trajectory of a child’s life through strengths-based advocacy and life-affirming connections.

##  From the U.S. Department of Justice, children from fatherless homez

## About CFF

The Center for Fathers and Families (CFF) is a 501(c)(3) non-profit agency with a strong history of responding to the needs of fathers and their families by offering programs and services that lead to family growth, enrichment, and empowerment.

[Learn More](https://www.cffsacramento.org/who-we-are)

![](https://static.wixstatic.com/media/2143c7_4438f63b30a44448ac1cda8193f2e515~mv2.jpg/v1/fill/w_56,h_37,al_c,q_80,usm_0.66_1.00_0.01,blur_2,enc_avif,quality_auto/2143c7_4438f63b30a44448ac1cda8193f2e515~mv2.jpg)

## Upcoming Events to put on your calendar

CFF also hosts events each in order to raise revenue and awareness to further our mission to strengthen families and build communities.  Each year, we are fortunate to have supportive community partners who share our vision. Their commitment to the clients we serve inspire us each and every day.

[New Events Coming Soon](https://www.cffsacramento.org/events)

![](https://static.wixstatic.com/media/2143c7_932729c3ef074bb998b457443868b0be~mv2.jpg/v1/fill/w_99,h_66,al_c,q_80,usm_0.66_1.00_0.01,blur_2,enc_avif,quality_auto/2143c7_932729c3ef074bb998b457443868b0be~mv2.jpg)

## HOW YOU CAN HELP

Our families often struggle with basic necessities. We strive to be flexible for our families & supporters.  We understand many donors have their own reasons for giving. We serve over 2500 youth through our programs.

[Help Now](https://www.cffsacramento.org/donate)

## View Our Programs

In order to strengthen the community, CFF serves all members of the family.

​

We have Adult, Youth and Health & Wellness programs available.

[View Programs](https://www.cffsacramento.org/copy-of-programs-services)

![](https://static.wixstatic.com/media/2143c7_ae85df7703e14949abafd97e67edaea4~mv2.jpg/v1/fill/w_109,h_73,al_c,q_80,usm_0.66_1.00_0.01,blur_2,enc_avif,quality_auto/2143c7_ae85df7703e14949abafd97e67edaea4~mv2.jpg)

![189854610\_4154879337902950\_6272856922304641843\_n.jpg](https://static.wixstatic.com/media/2143c7_2f073d8f2cf94cad9708fa03c79f5aa0~mv2.jpg/v1/fill/w_111,h_83,al_c,q_80,usm_0.66_1.00_0.01,blur_2,enc_avif,quality_auto/2143c7_2f073d8f2cf94cad9708fa03c79f5aa0~mv2.jpg)

## Many Years Ago...

At the Center for Fathers & Families

Many years ago at the Center for Fathers and Families, we began our Daddy's Here fatherhood support program. This photo is from our very first gathering in the park with single fathers and children. Two of the fathers have gone on to heaven and three of the fathers are still with the program. There is so much strength, love, bonding, and success in this photo.  #daddyshere #Dadicated #fatherhood #dadsmatter 

## TESTIMONIALS.

We donate to CFF because we believe in their cause, and we see how our funds helped the families that need it most.

Helping our local community is priceless. 

— Tory & Jan D.

I love volunteering with Center for Fathers and Families! This organization, walks the walk and is really making a difference.

— J.C 

Incredible people, organized, devoted, and caring! Making a difference! I personally seen the courses change lives.

— Mary R.

CFF is such an amazing non-profit! They truly change lives and make meaningful differences in the community. Growing up without a dad, I realized this is an organization that is vital and I could stand behind them all day.

— James T

# Where Children Feel Safe, Supported, and Empowered

CTC is dedicated to supporting children and families by focusing on what truly makes a difference. We believe every child deserves the right support and access to care to reach their full potential.

![Therapist-picture-flipped](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20700%20620%22%3E%3C/svg%3E "Therapist-picture-flipped")

![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20210%20140%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)

![fire-fighters](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20500%20500%22%3E%3C/svg%3E "fire-fighters")

![reading](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20500%20500%22%3E%3C/svg%3E "reading")

![children](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20500%20500%22%3E%3C/svg%3E "children")

![Three small children sitting on a tire swing together giggling](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20500%20500%22%3E%3C/svg%3E "img-05")

## Making a Difference

CTC still has **over 200 kids in need** who are waiting for services. Your donation will increase access to early intervention for all kids.

 

## With the community’s support, last year CTC provided a record breaking amount of services to kids in need

## 3,100

children and families supported through trusted care and services

## 137

free developmental play groups

## 1,934

families received free resources and case management

## 1,500

free developmental screenings

## In This Section

# Home

pause slideshowplay slideshow

Welcome to Encina High School!

Home of the Bulldogs

Expanding culturally relevant learning opportunities for students

We continue to strengthen our commitment to student success through meaningful partnerships that support both academic growth and social-emotional development. 

[Learn more!](https://encina.sanjuan.edu/news/news-detail/~board/encina-news/post/one-encina-many-voices)

Gaining real world teaching experience!

For a half hour each week, students in our education CTE pathway trade in their desks for first-grade tables to teach art and science to Greer's youngest learners!

[Learn more here!](https://encina.sanjuan.edu/news/news-detail/~board/encina-news/post/one-encina-many-voices-encina-cte-students-gain-real-world-teaching-experience-at-greer)

Encina Staff 2025-26

Encina staff members are ready for a SUPER school year!

Year-round sports opportunities!

We offer a wide range of sports teams for all seasons including football, basketball, soccer, track and more!

[Learn more!](https://encina.sanjuan.edu/athletics/list-of-sports-by-season)

Construction pathway observes campus upgrades!

During an on-campus visit, students toured the framing of the new administration building and observed the progress on the foundation of our future science building!

[Learn more!](https://encina.sanjuan.edu/news/news-detail/~board/encina-news/post/one-encina-many-voices-construction-pathway-students-explore-campus-improvements)

Encina's Campus Modernization is Underway!

A campus modernization is underway at Encina High School, which includes interior renovations of all classrooms and modernization of the cafeteria, administrative building, locker rooms and library. Existing classroom buildings O and Q will be demolished to make room for new science classrooms.

[Learn More](/fs/pages/28477)

Welcome to Encina High School!

Through project-based learning, our students are collaborating and exploring their identities and interests all while buiilding skills that they will carry with them into the future.

Encina High School Graduation

Congratulations Encina High School Class of 2026!

The San Juan Unified School District Board of Education is committed to equal opportunity for all individuals in district programs and activities. District programs, activities and services shall be free from unlawful discrimination, harassment (including sexual harassment), intimidation, and/or bullying based on actual or perceived characteristics of race; color; ancestry; nationality; national origin; immigration status; ethnic group identification; ethnicity; age; religion; pregnancy, childbirth, termination of pregnancy, or lactation, including related medical conditions and recovery; parental, family, or marital status; reproductive health decision-making; physical or mental disability; medical condition; sex (including sexual harassment); sex stereotypes; sex characteristics; sexual orientation; gender; gender identity; gender expression; veteran or military status; genetic information; affiliation with the Boy Scouts of America; a perception of one or more such characteristics; or association with a person or group with one or more of these actual or perceived characteristics. # We are Breakthrough T1D

As we drive toward curing type 1 diabetes, we help make everyday life better for the people who face it.

## Improving Lives and Driving Cures

This is more than a moment—it’s a movement. We connect the brightest minds to help advance treatments, influence policy, and improve access to care for those all over the world who need it. No organization does more to improve lives and drive toward cures for type 1 diabetes.

Today, we are leading the way to more effective solutions. Tomorrow, we will make T1D a thing of the past!

[Learn more about our impact](/our-impact/)

### We are Breakthrough T1D

At Breakthrough T1D (formerly JDRF), we’re bringing our 50+ year history with us while aligning our brand with who we are today.

No organization does more to improve everyday life with type 1 diabetes—from driving millions of dollars of global investment in therapy development and access, to advancing research, advocacy, and community support.

[Learn About Our Evolved Brand](https://www.breakthrought1d.org/about-breakthrought1d/)

# Helping kids thrive!

Roseville Kiwanis is an amazing organization committed to helping where help is needed, especially when children are concerned. We invite anyone with a heart for service to consider joining us in our efforts to serve the children and communities of Roseville, Rocklin, and Granite Bay. We meet at noon on Thursdays at the Pacific Street Café in Old Town Roseville. The invitation is always open. Stop in and see us some time.  

[Get Involved](contact-us-1)[Donate](javascript:void\(null\))

#### Our Mission

Our mission is to inspire and enable all young people, especially those who need us most, to reach their full potential as productive, caring, and responsible citizens.  
 

#### Great Futures Vision

Our vision is to provide a world-class Club Experience that assures success is within reach of every young person who enters our doors, with all members on track to graduate from high school with a plan for the future, demonstrating good character and citizenship, and living a healthy lifestyle.

#### About Us

##### Boys & Girls Clubs of Greater Sacramento provides a safe place for our region's youth to learn, grow, and thrive. Together with our generous supporters and dedicated community partners, we provide kids and teens with life-changing programs to support their academic, physical, and emotional development. We work hard to ensure that every young person that walks through our doors graduates on time, is living a healthy lifestyle, and demonstrates good character and citizenship.

###### Boys & Girls Clubs of Greater Sacramento is a tax-exempt public charity (federal tax ID #68-0338324). All contributions are tax-deductible to the extent allowable by law.   
  
[Donor Policies](https://bgcsac.org/how-to-help/donor-policy.html)

![](https://cdn.firespring.com/images/5cb2a4b3-06ce-4acb-a3b9-8c8930c13d6a.jpg)

© 2026 Boys and Girls Clubs of Greater Sacramento

[Privacy Policy](#privacy-policy)

## Privacy Policy

1.  **What Information Do We Collect?** When you visit our website you may provide us with two types of information: personal information you knowingly choose to disclose that is collected on an individual basis and website use information collected on an aggregate basis as you and others browse our website.
2.  **Personal Information You Choose to Provide** We may request that you voluntarily supply us with personal information, including your email address, postal address, home or work telephone number and other personal information for such purposes as correspondence, placing an order, requesting an estimate, or participating in online surveys. If you choose to correspond with us through email, we may retain the content of your email messages together with your email address and our responses. We provide the same protections for these electronic communications that we employ in the maintenance of information received by mail and telephone.
3.  **Website Use Information** Similar to other websites, our site may utilize a standard technology called "cookies" (see explanation below, "What Are Cookies?") and web server logs to collect information about how our website is used. Information gathered through cookies and server logs may include the date and time of visits, the pages viewed, time spent at our website, and the sites visited just before and just after ours. This information is collected on an aggregate basis. None of this information is associated with you as an individual.
4.  **How Do We Use the Information That You Provide to Us?** Broadly speaking, we use personal information for purposes of administering our business activities, providing service and support and making available other products and services to our customers and prospective customers. Occasionally, we may also use the information we collect to notify you about important changes to our website, new services and special offers we think you will find valuable. The lists used to send you product and service offers are developed and managed under our traditional standards designed to safeguard the security and privacy of all personal information provided by our users. You may at any time to notify us of your desire not to receive these offers.
5.  **What Are Cookies?** Cookies are a feature of web browser software that allows web servers to recognize the computer used to access a website. Cookies are small pieces of data that are stored by a user's web browser on the user's hard drive. Cookies can remember what information a user accesses on one web page to simplify subsequent interactions with that website by the same user or to use the information to streamline the user's transactions on related web pages. This makes it easier for a user to move from web page to web page and to complete commercial transactions over the Internet. Cookies should make your online experience easier and more personalized.
6.  **How Do We Use Information Collected From Cookies?** We use website browser software tools such as cookies and web server logs to gather information about our website users' browsing activities, in order to constantly improve our website and better serve our users. This information assists us to design and arrange our web pages in the most user-friendly manner and to continually improve our website to better meet the needs of our users and prospective users. Cookies help us collect important business and technical statistics. The information in the cookies lets us trace the paths followed by users to our website as they move from one page to another. Web server logs allow us to count how many people visit our website and evaluate our website's visitor capacity. We do not use these technologies to capture your individual email address or any personally identifying information about you.
7.  **Notice of New Services and Changes** Occasionally, we may use the information we collect to notify you about important changes to our website, new services and special offers we think you will find valuable. As a user of our website, you will be given the opportunity to notify us of your desire not to receive these offers by clicking on a response box when you receive such an offer or by sending us an email request.
8.  **How Do We Secure Information Transmissions?** When you send confidential personal information to us on our website, a secure server software which we have licensed encrypts all information you input before it is sent to us. The information is scrambled en route and decoded once it reaches our website. Other email that you may send to us may not be secure unless we advise you that security measures will be in place prior to your transmitting the information. For that reason, we ask that you do not send confidential information such as Social Security, credit card, or account numbers to us through an unsecured email.
9.  **How Do We Protect Your Information?** Information Security -- We utilize encryption/security software to safeguard the confidentiality of personal information we collect from unauthorized access or disclosure and accidental loss, alteration or destruction. Evaluation of Information Protection Practices -- Periodically, our operations and business practices are reviewed for compliance with organization policies and procedures governing the security, confidentiality and quality of our information. Employee Access, Training and Expectations -- Our organization values, ethical standards, policies and practices are committed to the protection of user information. In general, our business practices limit employee access to confidential information, and limit the use and disclosure of such information to authorized persons, processes and transactions.
10.  **How Can You Access and Correct Your Information?** You may request access to all your personally identifiable information that we collect online and maintain in our database by emailing us using the contact form provided to you within the site structure of our website.
11.  **Do We Disclose Information to Outside Parties?** We may provide aggregate information about our customers, sales, website traffic patterns and related website information to our affiliates or reputable third parties, but this information will not include personally identifying data, except as otherwise provided in this privacy policy.
12.  **What About Legally Compelled Disclosure of Information?** We may disclose information when legally compelled to do so, in other words, when we, in good faith, believe that the law requires it or for the protection of our legal rights.
13.  **Permission to Use of Materials** The right to download and store or output the materials in our website is granted for the user's personal use only, and materials may not be reproduced in any edited form. Any other reproduction, transmission, performance, display or editing of these materials by any means mechanical or electronic without our express written permission is strictly prohibited. Users wishing to obtain permission to reprint or reproduce any materials appearing on this site may contact us directly.

[Terms & Conditions](#terms-conditions)

## Terms & Conditions

## Donation Refund Policy

We are grateful for your donation and support of our organization. If you have made an error in making your donation or change your mind about contributing to our organization please contact us. Refunds are returned using the original method of payment. If you made your donation by credit card, your refund will be credited to that same credit card.

## Automated Recurring Donation Cancellation

Ongoing support is important to enabling projects to continue their work, so we encourage donors to continue to contribute to projects over time. But if you must cancel your recurring donation, please notify us.

## California Apartment Association

The Voice for California’s Rental Housing Industry

![](https://caanet.org/u/2026/03/Survival-Fund-Header-1920x1180.jpg)

Rental Housing Survival Fund

### Two Important Efforts. One Purpose.

Protecting California’s rental housing industry requires action at the courts and at the ballot box.

Your donation fuels both legal action and political advocacy, two essential efforts that protect the future of California’s rental housing industry.

[Donate Today](https://caanet.org/landing/rental-housing-industry-survival-fund/)

![](https://caanet.org/u/2020/12/Woman-signing-form-or-forms-1024x329.jpeg)

**CAA rental forms help you**  
**manage with confidence**

CAA offers more than 200 industry-standard forms tailored to California rental law. Members can fill them out digitally, build branded packages, and even collect signatures with DocuSign. CAA forms support compliance at every step.

[Access Forms](https://caanet.org/compliance-forms/)

![](https://caanet.org/u/2026/03/Rental-Fees-1-scaled.jpeg)

[Learn More](https://caanet.org/education/classes/rental-housing-fees-the-big-picture/)

## [CAA Housing Provider Helpline](https://caanet.org/products-services/helpline/about/)

Highlights

**Question**: My tenant is in the middle of a one-year fixed term lease (it started Aug. 1, 2025). The **refrigerator and stove** were not included at move in, so the tenant provided their own. Now the tenant is requesting the owner supply both under the new law. Do these appliances need to be provided now, or when the lease term expires?

**[Answer: >>](https://caanet.org/helpline-highlight-refrigerator-and-stove-requirement/)**

**Question**: The property has never been in **Section 8**. Don’t landlords have to apply for this program before accepting applicants from this program? Do Section 8 tenants pay a security deposit? Do I have to participate in Section 8? How can someone with a voucher meet my screening criteria?

[**Answer: >>**](https://caanet.org/housing-choice-vouchers-section-8-and-source-of-income-discrimination/)

**Question**: My tenant is not agreeing to a pre-moveout walkthrough. He wants to schedule a **final walkthrough** after he gives the keys back. How can I enter the unit before the tenant moves out to see what work needs to be done?

**[Answer: >>](https://caanet.org/helpline-highlight-walkthrough-and-final-inspection-timing/)**

In the world of Camp Taylor, Fun Tackles Fear, Friendships Bloom Fast, and Scars are Badges of Honor!

![](https://static.wixstatic.com/media/faeb55_be8d9da4528e41f3b296b8adabb16fd9~mv2.webp/v1/fill/w_185,h_108,al_c,q_80,usm_0.66_1.00_0.01,enc_avif,quality_auto/faeb55_be8d9da4528e41f3b296b8adabb16fd9~mv2.webp)

![2024\_camptaylor\_teen-03598.jpg](https://static.wixstatic.com/media/bac078_9233180914a748beabce7f95edc796e4~mv2.jpg/v1/fill/w_147,h_98,al_c,q_80,usm_0.66_1.00_0.01,blur_2,enc_avif,quality_auto/bac078_9233180914a748beabce7f95edc796e4~mv2.jpg)

A non-profit medically supervised camp for  
children with heart disease and their families.

![ctwording.vertical.clear.png](https://static.wixstatic.com/media/bac078_30eab01cec284180837cd71c444c7aaa~mv2.png/v1/fill/w_114,h_660,al_c,q_85,usm_0.66_1.00_0.01,enc_avif,quality_auto/ctwording_vertical_clear.png)

### Our mission​

Camp Taylor is a nonprofit organization passionately dedicated to improving the lives of children living with heart disease. As one of only eight medically supervised heart camps in the nation, we serve a critically underserved population, children battling the number one birth defect: congenital heart disease.

​

Founded in 2002 by the Gamino Family in honor of their son Taylor, who was born with half a heart, Camp Taylor was born out of love, necessity, and a fierce belief that every child deserves the chance to experience joy, connection, and self-worth regardless of their medical challenges.

​

Our mission is simple yet powerful:  
To create positive, life-affirming experiences in the lives of children with heart disease.

Through medically safe and emotionally enriching camp programs for youth, teens, and families- all offered free of charge- we give children a place to belong, to grow, and to be celebrated not in spite of their condition, but because of their courage in living with it.

​

In a region where pediatric cardiology patients often go without specialized peer support, Camp Taylor stands as a vibrant and essential community. We are more than just a camp - we are a place where brave hearts come to heal, laugh, learn, and thrive.

​

#### MISSION:

##### NARPM exists to support and empower professionals to elevate the property management industry through professional development, advocacy, and community.

#### VISION:

##### Property management professionals will be recognized as the keystone of successful real estate investing.

NARPM® is the premier professional association designed for real estate professionals who know first-hand the unique challenges of managing single-family and small residential properties.

We offer an effective, professional learning environment for owners of property management companies and their employees.

[Learn more](https://www.narpm.org/about/)

## Northern Nevada Claims Association Past Events

## Past Events

**CE Lunch & Learn**  
Thursday, June 11, 2026, 11:45 AM to 1:00 PM PDT  
Location: [50 Artisan Means Way, Suite A, Reno, NV 89511](https://maps.app.goo.gl/8V6cd3Mx3ds9D34N7)  
**Topic:** **Nevada** **Claims Landscape: Priorities, Trends, & Path Forward**  
**​Presenter:** **Ned Gaines, Nevada Insurance Commissioner**  
**Includes 1 hour of CE for Nevada Insurance Professionals**  
Nevada Insurance CE Course # 28780​​ (1 hour Property & Casualty)  
Provider: Nevada Claims Association (152468)  
11:45: Lunch & Networking  
12:00-1:00 Presentation

Nevada Insurance Commissioner Ned Gaines addressed the current state of the insurance market in the Silver State. Drawing on his experience as both a regulator and a former claims professional, Commissioner Gaines discussed the shifting legislative environment, the impact of recent wildfire-related regulations, the Division’s expectations for fair claims handling, and modern challenges of AI-driven claims.

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/nv-gaines-ned-2025b.jpg?1771027126)

**Ned Gaines** was appointed Nevada’s Insurance Commissioner October 6, 2025, following his tenure as Acting Commissioner and Chief Deputy. With over 25 years of industry experience, Gaines previously spent 12 years in leadership at the Washington Office of the Insurance Commissioner and held various private-sector roles. He currently holds key leadership positions within the **NAIC**, including Secretary/Treasurer of the Western Zone and Chair of the Senior Issues Task Force for 2026. Academically, Gaines holds a master’s in public policy from Liberty University and a bachelor’s in business from Excelsior College, specializing in consumer protection and regulatory compliance.

* * *

* * *

**CE Lunch & Learn**  
Thursday, May 14, 2026, 11:45 AM to 1:00 PM PDT  
Location: [50 Artisan Means Way, Suite A, Reno, NV 89511](https://maps.app.goo.gl/8V6cd3Mx3ds9D34N7)  
**Topic:** **Ethics for Insurance Professionals**  
**​Presenter:** **Brian Foote,** **MBA, WRT, AMRT, CHC**  
**Includes 1 hour of CE for Nevada Insurance Professionals**  
Nevada Insurance CE Course # 28765​ (1 hour Ethics)  
Provider: Nevada Claims Association (152468)  
11:45: Lunch & Networking  
12:00-1:00 Presentation​

Trust in the insurance industry is critical for the financial safety of households, businesses, and the overall economy. Insurance ethics is a major factor in maintaining confidence in the insurance system. With a focus on the big picture, this course covers the importance of general ethical guidelines, motivations to be ethical, and specific ethical concerns for insurance professionals and claims settlement practices.  

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/brianfoote.jpg?1774390577)

**Brian Foote**  
Brian Foote has been involved with the disaster recovery industry for over 35 years. He is a National Account Manager at BELFOR Property Restoration where works closely with adjusters on large and small losses. He has lead BELFOR's Triage Team for large catastrophes from hurricanes to wildfires for over a decade. Brian gives presentations and training on topics from disaster recovery to insurance and risk management ethics to groups across the country.

* * *

* * *

**Lunch & Learn**  
Thursday, April 9, 2026, 11:45 AM to 1:00 PM PDT  
Location: [50 Artisan Means Way, Suite A, Reno, NV 89511](https://maps.app.goo.gl/8V6cd3Mx3ds9D34N7)  
**Topic:** **AI:** **Why Deep Expertise Matters More Than Ever in Claims**  
**​Presenter:** **Grace Hanson, Founder/ CEO of** Elysian, **first AI-native TPA for commercial insurance**  
**Includes 1 hour of CE for Nevada Insurance Professionals**  
Nevada Insurance CE Course # 28768​​ (1 hour Property & Casualty)  
Provider: Nevada Claims Association (152468)  
11:45: Lunch & Networking  
12:00-1:00 Presentation​

The presentation examined why domain expertise remains essential as AI transforms claims operations. Grace Hanson draws on 25+ years of claims leadership to explain why large language models, despite their capabilities, cannot replicate the outcome-based learning that experienced adjusters develop over thousands of claim files. The session explored how AI tools can augment human judgment without replacing it, and what claims professionals should consider when evaluating AI-enabled solutions.

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/gracehanson.jpeg?1771022516)

**Grace Hanson** is Founder and CEO of Elysian, an AI-native third-party administrator for complex commercial claims. With over 25 years of experience in US and global P&C insurance, she has held claims leadership roles at AIG, Allied World, Hiscox, Hippo, and Homesite. Throughout her career, she has driven innovation in claims operations, digital distribution, analytics, and cognitive computing.

**homas Lawson**  
Tom began working part-time as a private crash reconstruction consultant in 2014, while completing his law enforcement career with the Nevada Department of Public Safety (Now the Nevada State Police).  
He began his law enforcement career with the Nevada Highway Patrol, where he served for over twenty-one years, rising to the rank of Lieutenant. Upon promoting to the rank of Captain, Tom was assigned to the Division of Parole and Probation, where he was later appointed as the eighteenth Chief of the Parole and Probation Division.  
In April 2022, Tom retired from the Nevada State Police with over twenty-six years of service. Upon retirement, Tom started private reconstruction consulting work full time.  
Additionally, Tom was an adjunct instructor for ten years with the Northwestern University Center for Public Safety where he taught crash investigation and reconstruction related courses within the United States, Canada, Kuwait, and Botswana.

**Lunch & Learn**  
Thursday, February 12, 2026, 11:45 AM to 1:00 PM PDT  
Location: [50 Artisan Means Way, Suite A, Reno, NV 89511](https://maps.app.goo.gl/8V6cd3Mx3ds9D34N7)  
**Topic:** **UNR Dorm Explosion Recovery**  
**​Presenter:** **Amanda Windes, University of Nevada, Reno - Organizational Resiliency Manager**  
**Includes 1 hour of CE for Nevada Insurance Professionals**  
Nevada Insurance CE Course # 23713​ (1 hour Property & Casualty)  
Provider: Nevada Claims Association (152468)  
11:45: Lunch & Networking  
12:00-1:00 Presentation​

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/argenta-hall_1.jpg)

On 5 July 2019, a natural gas explosion in Argenta Hall at UNR led to over 21 agencies responding for immediate life safety. This course will cover the complex incident with cascading effects impacting students, staff, and the public. It will also address working extensively with insurance carriers and University Issues Management Team to ensure proper priorities, documentation, and procedures were maintained for safely and full reimbursement of University, student, and vendor claims. ​

* * *

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/amanda-windes_2.jpg)

**Amanda Windes**  
Since 2016, Ms. Windes has been creating a comprehensive emergency management and continuity program at the University of Nevada, Reno after over 14 years' experience in all-hazards emergency management for the Department of Defense. While at UNR Ms. Windes has implemented the Stop the Bleed Campaign on campus, joined National Intercollegiate Mutual Aid Agreement (NIMMA), responded to two Stafford Act Disaster Declarations for severe winter storms, flooding and mudslides impacting Northern Nevada in 2017, has been part of numerous wildfire support operations, oversaw the response, recovery and continuity operations for the Argenta and Nye Hall Explosions in 2019, and deployed as part of the state's incident management team to support Lincoln County Area 51 Event 17-21 Sept 2019.  
       Ms. Windes is a member of the International Association of Emergency Managers (IAEM), Disaster Resilient Universities (DRU) and Nevada Emergency Preparedness Association (NEPA) as the Higher Education Committee Chair and State Certification Board Member. She is an alumna of the FEMA National Executive Emergency Management Academy and FEMA Advanced Academy.

This presentation offered a practical overview of wildfire behavior and proven strategies to reduce risk in communities. For insurance adjusters, it is essential to understand not only what a fire destroyed, but also why certain homes and neighborhoods were lost—and why others survived.  
​

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/megan.jpg?1759898316)

**​Megan Kay** is the Manager of the Living With Fire (LWF) Program and has been with the program since January 2020. In this role, she has worked extensively to help communities across Nevada become more fire adapted and has developed resources that bridge the gap between science and action to address the challenges of living with wildfire. Prior to joining LWF, Megan served as a wildland firefighter for five seasons in Carson City, Virginia City, and Incline Village, and later earned a Bachelor of Arts in fine arts and graphic design. She is passionate about serving her community and helping people live more safely with wildfire.

**Antonio A. (Tony) Rucci**  
Counterintelligence Special Agent (Retired)  
CyberSecurity Consult / Computer Crimes Investigator  
  
Antonio A. Rucci is a retired Counterintelligence Special Agent and US Army Warrant Officer, with more than 41 years of technical security experience. Tony runs a private CyberSecurity and Incident Response Consulting practice in Loudon, TN performing Penetration Testing, Passive Network Assessments, and Incident Response Professional Services to both private government and commercial entities throughout the country. **Lunch & Learn**  
Thursday, October 9, 2025, 11:45 AM to 1:00 PM PDT  
Location: [50 Artisan Means Way, Suite A, Reno, NV 89511](https://maps.app.goo.gl/8V6cd3Mx3ds9D34N7)  
**Topic:** **Disaster Damage Assessments** **​**

**Presenter:** **Kelly Echeveria, Washoe County Emergency Manager**  
**Included 1 hour of CE for Nevada Insurance Professionals**  
Nevada Insurance CE Course # 23686​, Category: Property & Casualty  
Provider: Nevada Claims Association (152468)  
  
The course provides guidelines for initial damage assessments following a disaster and the role they play in recovery efforts, insurance coverages, and government assistance. Participants will be able to: define the purpose of damage assessments, define basic terms and concepts, related to damage assessments, understand critical infrastructures, and be able to apply the basic principles of damage assessments following a disaster.

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/kelly.jpg?1756163966)

**Kelly Echeveria** is the Washoe County Emergency Management Administrator. Her responsibilities include leading Washoe County in planning, training, responding, mitigation, and recovery from all hazards.  
She has responded to numerous incidents including Superstorm Sandy, home fires, wildland fires, flooding, winter storms, power outages, tornados, and civil unrest. She has also served during County, State, and Federally declared emergencies such as hurricanes, flooding, and numerous wildland fires affecting urban areas.  
She serves on the Local Emergency Preparedness Committee, the State Emergency Response Commission, the Nevada Emergency Preparedness Association, the International Association of Emergency Managers, and represents Washoe County on the State Homeland Security Commission.  
Ms. Echeverria has bachelor’s degrees in criminal justice and international Relations from Roanoke College and a master’s degree in Crisis and Emergency Management from the University of Nevada, Las Vegas. She is a FEMA Advanced Academy alumnus. She is a member of the International Association of Emergency Managers and the Nevada Emergency Preparedness Association. Ms. Echeverria serves IAEM on the Awards and Recognition Committee and as the Region 9 State Liaison.

Thursday, September 18, 2025, 11:45 AM to 1:00 PM PDT

**Topic:** **Mediator’s Tips on Effective Negotiations**   
**Presenter:** **Charlie Burcham,** Arbitrator-Mediator-Special Master  
**Includes 1 hour of CE for Nevada Insurance Professionals**  
Nevada Insurance CE Course # 28448, Category: Property & Casualty  
Provider: Nevada Claims Association (152468)  
  
This course covers the critical role of effective negotiations in the claims settlement process and explores practical concepts that lead to successful negotiations, principles of contract law, and strategies to overcome common obstacles. Attendees will gain knowledge and skills to achieve win-win outcomes in both third-party and first-party claims.

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/attachment-1.jpeg?1756161251)

**Charlie Burcham**  
Upon graduating from the University of Washington Law School in 1985, Charlie Burcham fled the gloomy Pacific Northwest and has practiced law in Nevada ever since.   
Over the years, Charlie primarily toiled away as a civil litigator in Northern Nevada, trying many jury trials and handling a number of appellate matters before the Nevada Supreme Court and the Ninth Circuit Court of Appeals.    
In the early 1990’s, Charlie was in the first class of approved arbitrators under Nevada’s Court Annexed Arbitration Program, and since then has handled hundreds of arbitration assignments in several different districts. Fifteen years ago, Charlie became involved in other aspects of ADR, including private arbitrations and mediations. Additionally, over the years, Charlie has been appointed Special Master in a number of construction and insurance related lawsuits in various Northern Nevada jurisdictions. He is a member of the National Academy of Distinguished Neutrals.  
Currently, Charlie works as a private mediator and arbitrator, Special Master and Hearing Officer for the Nevada Medical Licensing Board. He resides in West Reno with his wife Colleen, 175-pound Newfoundland Dog named Guinness (who is a regional celebrity), 18 chickens and various other guests, squatters, pets and an occasional bear or two that wander down from the hills in the fall and consume his apples and pears. There is rarely a dull moment in Charlie’s household.

* * *

* * *

**Margaret Malzahn** works for Davies Claims Solutions as the Workers’ Compensation Claims Manager in Reno, NV. Margaret has over 15 years of workers’ compensation claims handling experience in multiple jurisdictions, including national accounts, handling both public and private sector claims. She has been working with Davies Claims Solutions for seven years and is licensed in the State of Nevada to handle  
workers’ compensation claims. Professionally, Margaret has a variety of experiences that lends to her ability to supervise and effectively assist her staff in drilling down into challenging claims issues. This experience includes United States Navy Hospital Corpsman, Program Management Assistant/Master Scheduler and Medical Staff Credentialing Specialist. She is a practitioner of the belief that learning is a lifelong activity; she has achieved a Bachelor of Science in Criminal Justice from Lynn University Boca Raton, FL; Master of Science in Human Resource Management from Capella University Minneapolis, MN; and a Doctorate in Law from Perdue Global University. Recently, she attained her certification in Project Management through UC Berkley.  
  
**Who should attend:** Claims adjusters, Human Resource Managers, Risk Managers, Supervisors/Mangers who are involved in the work injury process and prevention.

**Lunch & Learn**  
Thursday, June 12, 2025, 11:45 AM to 1:00 PM PDT  
Location: 50 Artisan Means Way, Suite A, Reno, NV 89511  
**Topic:** **Nevada Insurance Legislative Update**  
**Presenter:** Scott Kipper, Nevada Insurance Commissioner  
**Includes 1 hour of CE for Nevada Insurance Professionals**  
Nevada Insurance CE Course # 28188, Category: Property & Casualty  
Provider: Nevada Claims Association (Provider # 152468)

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/scott-kipper.jpg?1748384307)

​**Course description**  
Overview of legislative updates affecting insurance and the the process of claims adjusting, as well as challenges and trends moving forward.  
   
**Course Objectives**  
Participants will gain an understanding of recent legislative changes, future trends, insurance challenges unique to Nevada, and tools and resources to allow them to better perform their duties as insurance professionals.  

​**Instructor**: Scott Kipper, Nevada Insurance Commissioner  
Scott Kipper was appointed as Nevada’s Insurance Commissioner effective Feb. 27, 2023.  
As Commissioner, Kipper is charged with protecting consumers’ rights and the public’s interest in dealings with the insurance industry.  
Commissioner Kipper has more than 30 years of experience in the insurance industry, including prior stints as Nevada Insurance Commissioner from 2008 to 2010 and again from 2011 to 2015. He most recently served as the Deputy Commissioner for Operations for the State of Washington Office of the Insurance Commissioner. Prior to that, he founded a private consulting firm providing technical expertise on insurance issues, serving clients nationwide. He also has served as the Insurance Administrator of the Oregon Insurance Division, Deputy Commissioner at the Office of Health Insurance for the Louisiana Department of Insurance (DOI), and Senior Regional Director for State Affairs at America’s Health Insurance Plans (AHIP).  
Commissioner Kipper earned a bachelor’s degree in business administration from the University of Wyoming.

##  Thursday, March 5, 2020 2-hour CE Luncheon **at South Valleys Library**

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/documents_1.jpg)

**Topic: Document Recovery & Restoration**  
**Presenters**:  
**Steven Korasidas**, Executive General Adjuster, McLarens, Scottsdale, AZ  
**Brian Foote**, National Document Recovery Team, BELFOR Property Restoration, Reno, NV  
​  
**​Course Description:** Documents are the most valuable and irreplaceable assets, but they often become an under-appreciated risk during a large or small disaster. Leveraging lessons learned from real-world events including Hurricane Katrina, Hurricane Sandy, the 2014 east coast deep freezes, and the University of Kansas library fire, this presentation will help attendees identify documents affected by the loss, guard against security breaches and HIPAA violations, protect against further damage, know the options available to recover and restore various types of physical and electronic documents, and verify that contaminated documents are safe for future handling.  
  
**Approved for 2 hours of CE for Nevada Insurance Adjusters**  
Course Name: Document Recovery and Restoration         Course Number: 23687  
Provider: 152468 – Nevada Claims Association, P.O. Box 17152, Reno, NV 89511  
Course Category: Property                                          Approved Credit Hours: 2.00

## Thursday, October 3, 2019

South Valleys Public Library  
**Topic:** Construction Defects, presented by   
**Presenter:** Danny Sommers, Principal and Forensic Investigator at Farr West Engineering ​

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/danny-sommers_1.jpg)

Danny Sommers has broad-based experience in the fields of mechanical, civil and environmental engineering. In addition to overseeing many large-scale construction, agricultural, and manufacturing projects, his background includes extensive involvement with estimating, funding, permitting, and National Environmental Policy Act (NEPA) clearance. Danny routinely works with Federal, State and local entities to ensure that projects are compliant with government requirements. Danny provides investigative and expert witness services to insurance companies and the legal community on a wide variety of issues, including structural failures, automobile accident reconstruction, fire and explosion investigations and product defects analysis. Danny earned his bachelor's degree in mechanical engineering from the University of Nevada Reno and is a certified fire and explosion investigator. Danny is a member of the American Society of Mechanical Engineers and the Northern Nevada Claims Association. He loves the outdoors and enjoys the mountains, rivers, lakes, and wildlife of Nevada. He is a Reno native, and he and his wife Robin have enjoyed raising their four children in northern Nevada. Danny is a licensed pyrotechnician, and in his spare time provides pyrotechnic displays for the Reno Aces, Pops on the River, Pro Bull Riding, the Reno Rodeo and other local events.​

**​****May 3, 2018 Lunch & Learn  
**Our luncheon included three great presentations on restoring and recycling damaged property.  
  
**Presenter:** **Scott Bonnenfant of CRDN**  
**Topic: Textile and Electronics Restoration**  
  
**Presenters:** **Erich Schmitt and Trista Begovich of Lifecycle Solutions  
​****​****Topic: Electronics Recycling** **Presenter: Kendra Kostelecky of Waste Management**  
**Topic: Recycling**  

[![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/crdn.png?1524247584)](http://www.crdn.com/en-us/Pages/Why-CRDN.aspx)

CRDN removes odor, soot, grime, mold and mildew from clothing, bedding, rugs, leathers, suedes, furs, draperies, linens, stuffed animals, shoes, hats, belts, purses, and now performs electronics restoration.

[![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/413896286_orig.png)](http://lifecycle.solutions)

Lifecycle Solutions is an electronics recycling company that offers solutions to safely and securely destroy confidential data, manage assets and help companies become environmentally responsible with their electronic waste.

* * *

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/discovery-technician.jpg?1518712449)

**April 5, 2018 Lunch & Learn at South Valleys Public Library  
Topic:** 4 Myths and 1 Terrifying Truth About Cybercrime  
**Presenter: Ira Victor, Digital Forensic Practitioner at DiscoveryTechnician**  
  
Ira shared valuable information and practical solutions for protecting businesses and individuals from cybercrime. He dissected the myths about these crimes, the so-called silver bullets, and the piles of money being spent -- and lost -- in the battle to contain this menace. And the terrifying truth: If you've had any dealing with anyone who's hit with this type of fraud, you will be ensnared in litigation.

## 10th Annual Charity Bowling Tournament - March 15, 2018 - 2:45-5:00pm

[![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/cliff-bowling-leprechaun.jpg?1522693379)](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/cliff-bowling-leprechaun_orig.jpg)

The Northern Nevada Claims Association's Annual Charity Bowling Tournament was a great success. We had 19 bowling teams, pizza and drinks, great St. Patrick's Day costumes, fantastic Raffle Prizes, and everyone had a really wonderful time! Proceeds benefited both CARE Chest and the South Valleys Library. The librarians joined us and their bowling team had great costumes, and the new Director of CARE Chest also attended. Thanks to everyone for their  participation!  
  
Watch for the 2019 Tournament when the time comes and join the fun!

Location: South Valleys Public Library, 15650 Wedge Parkway, Reno, NV 89511  
Thursday February 1, 2018 • 11:45am-1:00pm  

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/570551592.jpg?250)

**Topic: Living more Safely with the Threat of Wildfire**  
**Presenter: Ed Smith, Living With Fire Program**  
  
Ed Smith is a natural resource specialist with University of Nevada Cooperative Extension, and co-manager of the Living With Fire Program. ## 2017 Holiday Luncheon  

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/nca-holiday-luncheon.gif?1512701065)

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/cliff-the-elf140.jpg?1512701041)

Our **Holiday Luncheon and Food Drive** to benefit **Evelyn Mount's Community Outreach** was relaxing and enjoyable. It was held at the South Valleys Library and we invited to library staff to dine with us. The Library Director shared how the funds donated by the chapter from the bowling tournament are being used to benefit library patrons. It was a great Turkey Luncheon with all the trimmings, interesting conversations, and we collected several frozen turkeys and a great deal of canned goods for those in need in our community.
