## November 2, 2017 Lunch & Learn

Location: South Valleys Public Library, 15650 Wedge Parkway, Reno, NV 89511  
Thursday November 2, 2017 • 11:45am-1:00pm

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/nnca-lunch-learn-banner.jpg?1489597139)

**Topic: Panel Discussion on New Technology and its Applications to Claims  
Panelists: Jim Smith, Brandon Lewis, Andrew Woodard, Peggy Willard Ross, and Gennady Stolyarov**  
This knowledgeable panel lead a discussion on new technology and its application to claims - including big data and autonomous vehicles - some examples of how the adjuster, insured and broker can work together, ethics, new legislation, and developments in education programs for claims professionals.

Presenter: Dean Stanphill, PE, CEM  
  
Location: South Valleys Public Library,** 15650 Wedge Parkway  
Reno, NV 89511  
  
**Date: Thursday September 7, 2017     Time: 11:45am-1:00pm**  
   
Mr. Dean Stanphill, PE, CEM is a Senior Manager with ES Engineering Services and manages the Reno office.  He has over 30 years experience with 20 years in Northern Nevada.  He has served as an expert witness on various landfill issues related to environmental compliance in Ohio, Nevada, California, and Melbourne Australia.

## June 1, 2017 Lunch & Learn  

**Topic: Disaster Recovery for Adjusters - 1 California CE Hour for Adjusters  
  
Presenter: Brian Foote, WRT, HCC, MBA**  
   
This class presents insurance adjusters with disaster recovery best practices and real-world examples to speed recovery time, recover damaged structures and contents, minimize secondary damage, and reduce overall risks and costs. Brian is on the national document restoration team for BELFOR Property Restoration and has participated in cleanup efforts after Hurricane Sandy and other large-loss events. 

* * *

## May 4, 2017 Lunch & Learn  

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/faa-symbol_orig.jpg)

**Topic: Drones  
Presenter: Drone Experts from the FAA and Reno Airport Authority  
**Drones allow exciting possibilities. Can they be useful for claims? Can they cause claims to be filed? The FAA will provide an introduction and overview of the rules and regulations regarding the use of drones for commercial purposes, focusing on how to obtain airspace access and operate safely in Truckee Meadows and the surrounding communities.  Reno Air Traffic Control personnel will demonstrate and explain the different rules in the airspace comprising approach and departure paths of aircraft operating in and around northern Nevada and California.  

* * *

* * *

## April 6, 2017 Lunch & Learn  

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/editor/cody-johnson.jpg?1489597252)

**Topic: Controlling Worker's Compensation Losses  
Presenter: Cody Johnson, Broker at Balsinger Insurance**  
Cody Johnson graduated from the University of Nevada with a degree in Engineering Physics.  After working in commercial development as a risk manager, Cody joined Balsiger Insurance in 2011 to help companies with their own risk management needs.  He helps clients control costs by detailing for them the drivers of insurance premium for their given industry.   
This is done by helping clients understand their experience modification, developing a return-to-work and light-duty programs, as well as considering how to prevent or minimize losses before they happen.  Topics for presentation will include these with a focus on controlling worker's compensation losses and an analysis of how these losses are handled can be a significant driver of the rate a company will pay for insurance.

## December 1, 2016 Lunch & Learn with new Nevada Insurance Commissioner  

![Picture](/uploads/3/4/4/0/34401456/published/richardson.jpg?1483733090)

**Speaker: Barbara Richardson, Nevada Insurance Commissioner**  
Commissioner Richardson fielded questions from membership and provided a lively and extremely informative discussion that was greatly appreciated by all who attended. Prior to assuming the role of Nevada Insurance Commissioner, Barbara had been the NH Insurance Department's Director of Operations and Fraud for 12 years. She oversaw several units of the Department, including the Premium and Retaliatory Tax unit, the Criminal Fraud investigation unit, the Business unit and the Producer Licensing unit. Barbara is a graduate of Vassar College and has a law degree from the University of New Hampshire School of Law and a MBA  from Loyola University in Chicago. She has been an active member on the national level of the NAIC's Producer Licensing Task Force, the Producer Licensing Working group and the Anti-fraud Task Force. In addition to these duties, Barbara was an adjunct professor at the University of New Hampshire School of Law teaching Insurance law.

# Welcome to the Rotary Club of Chico

Are you an established professional who wants to make positive changes in your community and the world? Our club members are dedicated people who share a passion for community service and friendship. Becoming a Rotarian connects you with a diverse group who share your drive to give back.

Welcome to the Rotary Club of Chico

Rotary in Action

#### About Chico

Our club members are dedicated people who share a passion for both community service and friendship. Becoming a Rotarian connects you with a diverse group of professionals who share your drive to give back.

To become a new member, [contact us](https://chicorotary.com/singleemail/contactus) to get connected with a current Rotarian and learn about the membership process.

#### Rotary at a Glance

Rotary brings together a global network of volunteer leaders who dedicate their time and talent to tackle the world’s most pressing humanitarian challenges. Rotary connects 1.2 million members from more than 200 countries and geographical areas. Their work impacts lives at both the local and international levels.

#### Contact Us

Keep in touch, or reach out to us from via our social media websites!

[office@chicorotary.com](mailto:office@chicorotary.com)

(530) 345-1605

PO Box 11, Chico, CA 95927

Point West Rotary in the Community

[Message from President Randy Burton](Stories/message-from-president-randy-burton)

Dear Members and Fellow Rotarians,

Happy New Rotary Year!  It is with great excitement, humility, and gratitude that I welcome you to another year of fellowship, service, and opportunity.

A new Rotary year is more than a change in leadership—it's a chance to renew our commitment to the ideals that brings us together. Every meeting, every service project, and every life we touch reflects the spirit of Rotary and our shared commitment to **Service Above Self**.

Whether you're a member, a community partner, or a first-time visitor, there's a place for you in Rotary. We invite you to join us as we serve, connect, and create positive change—one project, one friendship, and one act of kindness at a time.

To our current members - thank you for placing your trust in me to serve as your Club President. I look forward to working alongside each of you as we continue making a positive difference in our community and beyond. 

This year, I hope each of us will find new ways to engage. Whether you've been a Rotarian for decades or you've just joined our club, your ideas, talents, and enthusiasm are what make our club thrive. Together, we can strengthen our friendships, grow our membership, expand our service, and continue creating lasting change.

Together, we can accomplish far more than any one of us could alone.  Here's to a wonderful Rotary year filled with friendship, service, laughter, and lasting memories!

Yours in Rotary,

**Randy Burton**

President - 2026-27

Rotary Club of Point West – Sacramento

![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Randy-Burton.jpg)

[A New Rotary Van for the Community!](Stories/a-new-rotary-van-for-the-community)

Collaboration works!  Three clubs in District 5180 came together to create a great project to bring transportation to youth in our community.  The Rotary Clubs of Point West Rotary, Arden Arcade and American River joined in purchasing a Rotary Van that is wrapped in Rotary branding.  More importantly, the passenger van has been donated to both the Fulton-El Camino and Mission Oaks Recreation and Park Districts to transport students to youth activities throughout the area.  As part of the unveiling, Rotarians also provided students from Aspire High School with a hands-on vehicle maintenance tutorial, covering basics such as checking tire pressure and tread, fluids, windshield wipers and how to change a tire. 

The presentation was captured by Channel 13 News featuring the current presidents of each of the Rotary Clubs involved - Dorthe Wright, Point West Rotary; Matt Ross, Arden Arcade Rotary; and Allison Hamaker, American River Rotary.

  ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Rotary-van_20260531-210159.jpg)  ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Good-Day---van-1_20260531-210540.JPG)

[Birdies Over Bunkers 2026](Stories/birdies-over-bunkers-2026)

![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Birdies-over-Bunkers-web-page-v2_20260415-194603.png)

    For more info or to register [CLICK HERE!](https://birdease.com/34357)    

[PWR's Annual Golf Tournament "Birdies Over Bunkers"](Stories/pwr-s-annual-golf-tournament-birdies-over-bunkers)

![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/image_20260318-234927.png)One of our favorite events of the year is coming up...and you won't want to miss this!  Join us on Monday, May 18, 2026 for our annual Point West Rotary charity golf event "Birdies Over Bunkers".  We'll be teeing off with a shotgun start at 11 am at the beautiful golf course at Valley Hi Country Club - a four-person scramble.   If you're a scratch golfer...or a once-in-a-while player...we need you!  All net proceeds go back to our local community...supporting the California Eagles Golf (a program under The First Tee of Sacramento) and other children's charities.  Have fun and support our local community at the same time!  We'll have lots of fun on-course events/contests, a drone ball drop, putting contest, lunch, dinner...and so much more!  It's easy to register...just click [HERE](https://birdease.com/birdiesoverbunkers) - register or get more info!

![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Birdies-Over-Bunkers-25.jpg)  ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Birdies-Over-Bunkers-4.jpg)   

![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Birdies-Over-Bunkers-61.jpg)  ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Birdies-Over-Bunkers-1.jpg)

[**PWR changing lives in Zambia!**](Stories/pwr-changing-lives-in-zambia!)

Enriching lives in Zambia, Africa - that's one of the key reasons that Point West Rotary has taken the lead in securing a Rotary Foundation Global Grant.  Funding from the global grant will provide reading education to thousands of young people in the community of South Luangwa, Zambia and it will help them achieve a level of literacy which can improve their chances of success in life.

![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/uyoba-front.jpg)  ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/inside-mfuwe-kids.jpg) 

![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/kids-in-classroom.jpg)  ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/outside-mhkanya.jpg)

By integrating structured reading support in the school system and ensuring long-term sustainability through local oversight, this initiative will help break the cycle of poverty, create economic opportunity and empower future generations in South Luangwa.  We invite all Rotary Clubs, both inside and outside of our district, to join us in changing lives and doing good in the world!  For more information, contact Mike Cendro, PWR International Grants Committee Chair, at [mtcendro@gmail.com](mailto:mtcendro@gmail.com)

[**PWR Supports Eagles Golf!**](Stories/pwr-supports-eagles-golf!)

Over the last several decades, Point West Rotary has proudly supported the California Eagles golf program at Haggin Oaks.  PWR has provided financial support over the years as well as having many members showing up on Tuesday nights (each week from May to September) at Haggin Oaks to be coaches to the many athletes in the program.   Our club recently finished out another year serving as coaches and we want to thank all of those that participated...PWR members **Russ Fujii, Robert Haney, Bea Maurer, Mike Bede, Kathi Berger, Sandy Thompson, Tom Engwer, Joanie Mather, Glen Cheron, DeWana Ljung, Desiree Wilson** as well as Ed Schatz, J.R. Corcoran, Jake Slaven, Zach Berger, Dwight Chambers. It was an amazing year and we think the coaches had as much fun as the athletes!

![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Eagles-Golf-1.jpg)    ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/eagles-golf-3.jpg)     ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Eagles-Golf-5.jpg)    ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Eagles-Golf-2.jpg)    ![](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/Images/Eagles-Golf-4.jpg)

The California Eagles is a golf program for athletes of all ages with special needs. The program includes, but is not limited to, those with Down’s Syndrome, Muscular Dystrophy, Cerebral Palsy, Multiple Sclerosis, Attention Deficit Disorder, autism, deafness, blindness, or recovering from heart attacks and strokes.

POINT WEST ROTARY - WHO WE ARE !

 

_Point West Rotary is a group of active members working_

_to improve our community and have fun while doing it!_

_We always welcome others_

_to join in our efforts to make an impact and change lives!_

**Our Mission**

_The Rotary Club of Point West Sacramento is a vibrant_

_community service organization_

_promoting service to those in need,_

_goodwill, peace, and fellowship_

**Our Vision**

_Be a major contributor to_

_children’s charities_

_and other local and global charitable opportunities,_

_and to be recognized for such_

_To read our document on our **Voice, Mission, Vision,**_

_**Brand and Core Values**_

[click here](https://clubrunner.blob.core.windows.net/00000002266/en-ca/files/homepage/2024-pwr-voice/2024-PWR-Voice.pdf)

## Where Leaders Meet to Serve.

The Rotary Club of Sacramento is the premiere service organization in the Sacramento region, where a large and diverse group of leaders gather to strengthen our community through service, the exchange of ideas, and fellowship. We welcome into membership leaders from diverse backgrounds at all stages of their careers who are dedicated to community service and committed to personal and professional growth.

[Be Our Guest](https://portal.clubrunner.ca/2010/Form/membership-inquiry)

## Local Project Highlights

![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==)

### Rotary in Sacramento

![Golf 4 Kids logo](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==)

The [Golf 4 Kids tournament](http://www.sacrotarygolf4kids.com/) provides approximately $35,000 in support each year for programs at five local schools. Funds go to provide medical services, braces and other mechanical aids necessary for the rehabilitation.

[Learn More](https://rotarysacramento.com/get-involved/golf-4-kids/ "Golf 4 Kids Tournament")

![Sacramento Century Challenge logo](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==)

The [Sacramento Century Challenge](http://www.sacramentocentury.com/) is the Rotary Club of Sacramento’s main fundraising event. In the past nine years, more than 200 volunteers have given hundreds of hours of service and have raised more than $619,000 for beneficiary charities!

[Learn More](https://rotarysacramento.com/get-involved/sacramento-century-challenge/ "Sacramento Century Challenge")

## International Project Highlight

### Safe Water Honduras

Rotary Club of Sacramento is teaming up with World Vision and other Rotary Clubs from across the region and around the world to make a huge difference in the lives of thousands of Hondurans, bringing clean water to the people of rural western Honduras

[LEARN MORE](https://rotarysacramento.com/get-involved/international-services-wash/ "World Community Service")

![World Map](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==)

### Rotary in the World

## Rotary’s Response to COVID-19

Our board of directors voted to focus our discretionary community grants funding on providing rental assistance to those who are newly in need due to financial challenges brought on by the pandemic. While we remain concerned about those who are currently unsheltered, especially with winter here, our goal is to invest the funds at our disposal to prevent more people from becoming homeless. Our motivation is the belief that the loss of shelter, possessions and dignity is vastly more costly to those individuals and to society than investing in them before becoming unsheltered.

After thoroughly vetting the local nonprofits who provide emergency rental assistance, our board approved our first grant of $2500 to Volunteers of America (VOA) to help Susan, the widow of a veteran who recently passed away, and her 19 year-old son. [Learn more about how the Rotary Club](https://rotarysacramento.com/covid-response-virtual-meeting-strategy/) is helping Susan and [how Rotary Club international](https://www.rotary.org/en/rotarys-response-coronavirus-pandemic) is responding to COVID.

![Rotary International Logo](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==)

## Working Together for Peace & Justice

At Rotary, we have no tolerance for racism. Promoting respect, celebrating diversity, demanding ethical leadership, and working tirelessly to advance peace are central tenets of our work.

We have more work to do to create more just, open and welcoming communities for all people.

We know there are no easy fixes and that challenging conversations and work lie before all of us. Rotary’s strength has long been our ability and commitment to bringing people together. We will tap into that strength now as we stand with those who are working for peace and justice.

Rotary will do our part to listen, learn and take action to ensure that we continue to contribute to making positive change.

![Rotary International Logo](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==)

![Create Lasting Impact](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==)

About Rotary Club of Sacramento & RCS Foundation

The Rotary Club of Sacramento is a non-profit association of business and community leaders serving the Sacramento region and enriching the professional and personal lives of its members. The Club’s mission is to be of service – in the community, in the workplace, and throughout the world. Rotary members and volunteers build goodwill and peace, provide humanitarian service, and encourage high ethical standards in all vocations. Gifts to the Rotary Club of Sacramento Foundation support the good works of the Club, and are tax-deductible as charitable contributions. The Foundation’s federal tax ID number is 23-7117223.

# Where Learning Comes Alive.

[Explore Our Programs](/20262027-programs)

[Schedule a Tour Now](https://calendar.google.com/calendar/u/0/appointments/schedules/AcZssZ3Ic9J0h40Dp1xB7cmq1kGsSLTLix_rUj11rB6mM_GehD4v3I8N8PO6fS_fGIMU17lmsjRgp7uY)

![Blue text displaying '33+' on a white background.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/bde31ca5-d07e-41fe-8dde-efa4a01944df/33.png)

### Years

#### Of  Progressive Education

![Number six in blue text on a white background.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/d4cee326-07ec-4b48-8d11-7c70a43c96bc/33+%282%29.png)

### Charter Partners

#### Approved Vendor

![Image of a clock showing the time 12:1 in large blue numbers.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/a2d8b66b-150e-4d8a-8b1c-545124d0865e/33+%281%29.png)

### Student to Teacher Ratio

#### Small Class Sizes; Big Impact

![A young boy in a blue t-shirt with a Mario design, holding a small snake. He is smiling and looking down at the snake. In the background, there are several other children and adults in a room with bright lighting and framed pictures on the wall.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/35e65db5-b5a6-4b91-a40f-f7ac746af51b/IMG_2514.jpeg)

Roseville Community School is more than a learning center — it's a thriving educational community where children are known by name, celebrated for who they are, and encouraged to grow at their own pace.

We offer flexible full-time and part-time programs for kindergarten through 6th grade, designed to complement your family's homeschool journey. As a certified vendor for six Placer County charter schools, we make it easy for charter families to use their educational funds to enroll.

Whether your child joins us two days a week or five, they'll experience hands-on, project-based learning rooted in social-emotional development, creativity, and genuine community.

# Why Families Choose RCS

[Learn More](/about)

### A Different Kind of Education

![Children playing in a backyard playground with a large black tube tunnel, a climbing structure with green ropes, and a wooden platform with chairs, surrounded by trees and a wooden fence, on an autumn day.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/ef89be3d-2860-4ad9-8fe9-ea6f9707a1c1/unnamed+%2830%29.jpg)

#### **Whole-Child Learning**

We nurture curiosity, creativity, and confidence alongside academics — because emotional well-being and academic growth go hand in hand.

![A woman and two children jumping into a lake from a dock on a sunny day.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/cfd23ac6-eeed-4cd0-977f-b778ba10d36e/unnamed+%2859%29.jpg)

#### **Community & Belonging**

Founded by families, built on connection. Our parent participation model and multi-age classrooms create lasting friendships and a true sense of belonging.

![Children participating in a color run event, crawling under colorful streamers on a pink tarp, with spectators in the background.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/173fd255-d5f1-4211-963c-fdb870629665/IMG_7410.JPG)

#### **Hands-On Exploration**

From cooking and gardening to STEAM projects and field trips, children learn by doing, not just listening.

![A group of children and an adult in a classroom are gathered around a table with a baking tray, making snacks. One child is holding a piece of salmon, and the adult is assisting. Other children are watching and participating. Books and educational materials are visible in the background.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/c5239f1f-abee-42cd-bf95-6c4a0f89b8a4/unnamed+%2824%29.jpg)

#### **Academic Excellence**

Our curriculum integrates Language Arts, Math, and Social Studies and Science through real-world, interdisciplinary projects anchored in Common Core standards and the Science of Reading.

Programs for Every Learner

-   ![A colorful illustration of a bird with wings spread wide, with a splash of rainbow colors behind it. The setting appears to be a sunny day.](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6a2c7145cd74e817f3abcdc4/51eeddec-dad8-47d5-9650-66b699cfa338/unnamed+%2813%29.jpg)

[How Charter Enrollment Works](/charter-school-vendor)

RCS is an approved educational vendor for six local charter schools. Charter families can use their student's educational funds to help cover the cost of classes — and most of our individual programs fit comfortably within a family's annual charter allotment. If needed, families are also welcome to pay out of pocket for any remaining balance.

## Enrolled in a Placer County Charter School?

Enrollment is flexible: families select the classes or programs that work for their child, submit vouchers monthly through their charter school, and the charter pays RCS directly.

We also make it easy to stay aligned with your Education Specialist. RCS provides weekly learning goal summaries and program updates, plus work samples you can share directly with your charter teacher — keeping your homeschool record complete and your ES informed.

# See RCS in Action

Take a peek inside our learning community. From outdoor play to hands-on projects, this is what learning looks like at RCS.

# Getting Started Is Easy

# 〰️

# Getting Started Is Easy 〰️ Getting Started Is Easy 〰️

-   ### Step 1 — Schedule a Tour
    
    Visit our campus, meet our team, and experience our child-centered environment firsthand.
    
-   ### Step 2 — Child Visit Day
    
    Your child spends a half-day with us — joining real class sessions, meeting teachers and classmates, and getting a feel for RCS life. Currently FREE!
    
-   ### Step 3 — Enroll
    
    If RCS feels like the right fit, we send you an enrollment contract and get your child set up in their program.
    
-   ### Step 4 — Begin Learning
    
    Your child joins their class and becomes part of our community. We're here every step of the way.

# SACRAMENTO SHERIFF'S POSSE

#### MISSION STATEMENT

#### MOTHERS AGAINST DRUNK DRIVERS (MADD)

#### OBJECTIVE STATEMENT

The Mission of The Sacramento Sheriff's Posse is to promote and enhance community service by offering equestrian activities through an organization of volunteers and qualified members interested in supporting our law enforcement community.

#### OBJECTIVE STATEMENT

#### MOTHERS AGAINST DRUNK DRIVERS (MADD)

#### OBJECTIVE STATEMENT

The Objective of The Sacramento Sheriff's Posse is to represent our Sheriff's Department  as approachable, caring and involved in our community and it's residents.

#### MOTHERS AGAINST DRUNK DRIVERS (MADD)

#### MOTHERS AGAINST DRUNK DRIVERS (MADD)

#### MOTHERS AGAINST DRUNK DRIVERS (MADD)

The Sacramento Sheriff's Posse proudly represents Mothers Against Drunk Drivers as well as the City and County of Sacramento, California.

### **_Making the most of Summer!_**

#### President's Message

![photo link of 2026 SCA President Melinda Galvan](photos/board/2026/MelindaGalvan_image001_2601.jpg)

##### 2026 SCA President  
Melinda Galvan

#### **Dear SCA Members,**

#### As summer unfolds, many of us find ourselves balancing packed schedules, family gatherings, vacations, and endless planning. Sometimes it feels like we need a vacation from the vacation!

#### Yet, in the middle of the hustle, we’re reminded of what matters most—quality time with family and friends and creating memories that last far longer than the to-do lists. Even life’s little quirks can make us smile. I don’t mind stepping into the shower, but somehow getting caught in the rain still feels like an inconvenience!

#### As your President, I hope you take time to slow down, enjoy the season, and appreciate both the planned adventures and the unexpected moments that make life memorable. Wishing you a safe, relaxing, and joy-filled summer!

## _Melinda_

#### [Melinda Galvan](emailto:mgalvan0328@gmail.com)

#### State Farm Insurance

#### 2026 SCA President

### SCA Mission Statement:

#### The Sacramento Claims Association is a professional organization fostering contact and communication among its members and others in the insurance claims industry.  The cornerstone of the Sacramento Claims Association is rooted in four primary areas:  
Education; Charitable; Communication; and Social.
